A PreToolUse hook blocks destructive SQL — DROP TABLE/COLUMN, TRUNCATE, DELETE/UPDATE without WHERE, irreversible ALTERs — in Bash commands or migration-file writes unless a migration plan is declared (.migration-plan file, or MIGRATION-SAFETY: ALLOW sentinel stating target, dry-run, rollback).
